\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\Delta;\Gamma\vdash s:{?}A\quad\Delta;a:A\vdash t:{\perp}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=125.3896pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-e${?}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 31.75171pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\Delta;\Gamma\vdash{t{\color[rgb]{0,0.6,0}\blacktriangleright}^{a}s}:{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}

We take:

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\begin{array}[]{c}\enskip{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\begin{array}[]{c}\enskip{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\vdash{{?}\Delta^{\perp},\Gamma^{\perp},{?}A}\quad\begin{array}[]{c}\enskip{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\begin{array}[]{c}\enskip{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\vdash{{?}\Delta^{\perp},A^{\perp},{\perp}}\quad\vdash{\text{1}}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=76.77777pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{l-cut}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 15.69447pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\vdash{{?}\Delta^{\perp},A^{\perp}}$}}}}\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=111.7778pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{l-!p}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 32.6389pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\vdash{{?}\Delta^{\perp},{!}{A^{\perp}}}$}}}}\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=210.36116pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{l-cut}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 72.31947pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\vdash{{?}\Delta^{\perp},{?}\Delta^{\perp},\Gamma^{\perp}}$}}}}\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox to245.36119pt{$\mathord{=}\mkern-6.0mu\leaders{\hbox{$\mkern-2.0mu=\mkern-2.0mu$}}{\hfill}\mkern-6.0mu\mathord{=}$}\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{l-?c}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 100.61118pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\vdash{{?}\Delta^{\perp},\Gamma^{\perp}}$}}}}\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=275.63898pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{l-${\perp}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 110.61118pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\vdash{{?}\Delta^{\perp},\Gamma^{\perp},{\perp}}$}}}}\end{array}

12.

m-i
{\perp}
. The derivation is:

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\Delta;\Gamma\vdash t:B\quad\Delta;\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash s:B^{\perp}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=118.30515pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-i${\perp}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 21.23952pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\Delta;\Gamma,\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash t\lightning s:{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}

We take:

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\begin{array}[]{c}\enskip{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\begin{array}[]{c}\enskip{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\vdash{{?}\Delta^{\perp},\Gamma^{\perp},B}\quad\vdash{{?}\Delta^{\perp},\Gamma^{\prime\perp},B^{\perp}}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=128.61798pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{l-cut}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 22.07289pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\vdash{{?}\Delta^{\perp},{?}\Delta^{\perp},\Gamma^{\perp},\Gamma^{\prime\perp}}$}}}}\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=163.61801pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{l-${\perp}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 34.43402pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\vdash{{?}\Delta^{\perp},{?}\Delta^{\perp},\Gamma^{\perp},\Gamma^{\prime\perp},{\perp}}$}}}}\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox to192.22913pt{$\mathord{=}\mkern-6.0mu\leaders{\hbox{$\mkern-2.0mu=\mkern-2.0mu$}}{\hfill}\mkern-6.0mu\mathord{=}$}\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{l-?c}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 59.53127pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\vdash{{?}\Delta^{\perp},\Gamma^{\perp},\Gamma^{\prime\perp},{\perp}}$}}}}\end{array}

13.

m-i
1
. The derivation is:

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{\kern 13.06947pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\vphantom{{}^{@}}$}}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=32.77786pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-i$\text{1}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\Delta;\cdot\vdash\star:\text{1}$}}}}\end{array}

We take:

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\begin{array}[]{c}\enskip{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{\kern 3.62495pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\vphantom{{}^{@}}$}}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=13.88882pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{l-$\text{1}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\vdash{\text{1}}$}}}}\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox to39.99995pt{$\mathord{=}\mkern-6.0mu\leaders{\hbox{$\mkern-2.0mu=\mkern-2.0mu$}}{\hfill}\mkern-6.0mu\mathord{=}$}\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{l-?w}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 2.26387pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\vdash{{?}\Delta^{\perp},\text{1}}$}}}}\end{array}

14.

m-e
1
. The derivation is:

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\Delta;\Gamma\vdash t:\text{1}\quad\Delta;\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash s:A$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=106.10385pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-e$\text{1}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 9.30548pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\Delta;\Gamma,\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash s{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{[}}\star:=t{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{]}}:A$}}}}\end{array}

We take:

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\begin{array}[]{c}\enskip{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\vdash{{?}\Delta^{\perp},\Gamma^{\perp},\text{1}}\quad\begin{array}[]{c}\enskip{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{\kern 5.13889pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\vdash{{?}\Delta^{\perp},\Gamma^{\prime\perp},A}$}}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=68.6666pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{l-${\perp}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\vdash{{?}\Delta^{\perp},\Gamma^{\prime\perp},A,{\perp}}$}}}}\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=159.19437pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{l-cut}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 31.38887pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\vdash{{?}\Delta^{\perp},\Gamma^{\perp},{?}\Delta^{\perp},\Gamma^{\prime\perp},A}$}}}}\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox to194.1944pt{$\mathord{=}\mkern-6.0mu\leaders{\hbox{$\mkern-2.0mu=\mkern-2.0mu$}}{\hfill}\mkern-6.0mu\mathord{=}$}\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{l-?c}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 59.68057pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\vdash{{?}\Delta^{\perp},\Gamma^{\perp},\Gamma^{\prime\perp},A}$}}}}\end{array}

Next we address completeness. By induction on the size of the cut-free derivation of
\vdash{\Gamma_{0}}
.

1.

l-ax
: The derivation ends in

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{\kern 14.30896pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\vphantom{{}^{@}}$}}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=35.25684pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{l-ax}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\vdash{B,B^{\perp}}$}}}}\end{array}

There are two cases depending on whether
A=B
or
A=B^{\perp}
.

(a)

A=B
. Then
\Gamma=B^{\perp}
and

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{\kern 28.9419pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\vphantom{{}^{@}}$}}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=64.5227pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-uax}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\cdot;a:B\vdash a:B$}}}}\end{array}

(b)

A=B^{\perp}
. Then
\Gamma=B
and

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{\kern 33.30304pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\vphantom{{}^{@}}$}}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=73.245pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-uax}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\cdot;a:B^{\perp}\vdash a:B^{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}

2.

l-
\otimes
: The derivation ends in

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\vdash{\Gamma_{1},B_{1}}\quad\vdash{\Gamma_{2},B_{2}}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=80.50694pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{l-$\otimes$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 3.05557pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\vdash{\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2},B_{1}\otimes B_{2}}$}}}}\end{array}

There are three cases depending on whether
A=B_{1}\otimes B_{2}
or
A\in\Gamma_{1}
or
A\in\Gamma_{2}
.

(a)

A=B_{1}\otimes B_{2}
. By the IH twice,
\cdot;\Gamma_{1}^{\perp}\vdash s_{1}:B_{1}
and
\cdot;\Gamma_{2}^{\perp}\vdash s_{2}:B_{2}
. We conclude from the following derivation

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\begin{array}[]{c}\text{IH }\\
\cdot;\Gamma_{1}^{\perp}\vdash s_{1}:B_{1}\end{array}\quad\begin{array}[]{c}\text{IH }\\
\cdot;\Gamma_{2}^{\perp}\vdash s_{2}:B_{2}\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=126.38213pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-i$\otimes$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 5.5557pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\cdot;\Gamma_{1}^{\perp},\Gamma_{2}^{\perp}\vdash\langle s_{1},s_{2}\rangle:B_{1}\otimes B_{2}$}}}}\end{array}

Thus it suffices to take
t:=\langle s_{1},s_{2}\rangle
.

(b)

A\in\Gamma_{1}
. Then
\Gamma_{1}=\Gamma_{3},A
.
Hence by IH there exist terms
s,r
such that:

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\begin{array}[]{c}\text{IH }\\
\cdot;\Gamma_{3}^{\perp},a:B_{1}^{\perp}\vdash s:A\end{array}\begin{array}[]{c}\enskip{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\begin{array}[]{c}\enskip{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{\kern 55.86778pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\vphantom{{}^{@}}$}}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=118.37448pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-ax}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\cdot;b:B_{1}^{\perp}\invamp B_{2}^{\perp}\vdash b:B_{1}^{\perp}\invamp B_{2}^{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}\begin{array}[]{c}\text{IH }\\
\cdot;\Gamma_{2}^{\perp}\vdash r:B_{2}\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=209.12578pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-e$\invamp_{2}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 42.92712pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\cdot;b:B_{1}^{\perp}\invamp B_{2}^{\perp},\Gamma_{2}^{\perp}\vdash b{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\blacktriangledown}r:B_{1}^{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=342.75511pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-sub}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 88.0346pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\cdot;\Gamma_{3}^{\perp},\Gamma_{2}^{\perp},b:B_{1}^{\perp}\invamp B_{2}^{\perp}\vdash s{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}a:=b{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\blacktriangledown}r{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}:A$}}}}\end{array}

so it suffices to take
t:=s{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}a:=b{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\blacktriangledown}r{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}
.

(c)

A\in\Gamma_{2}
. Then
\Gamma_{2}=\Gamma_{3},A
. Hence by IH there exist terms
s,r
such that:

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\begin{array}[]{c}\enskip{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\cdot;a:B_{1}^{\perp}\invamp B_{2}^{\perp}\vdash a:B_{1}^{\perp}\invamp B_{2}^{\perp}\quad\begin{array}[]{c}\text{IH }\\
\cdot;\Gamma_{1}^{\perp}\vdash r:B_{1}\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=184.16975pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-e$\invamp_{1}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 27.78827pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\cdot;a:B_{1}^{\perp}\invamp B_{2}^{\perp},\Gamma_{1}^{\perp}\vdash a\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bullet}\,r:B_{2}^{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}\quad\cdot;\Gamma_{3}^{\perp},b:B_{2}^{\perp}\vdash s:A$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=316.80487pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-sub}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 72.89578pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\cdot;\Gamma_{1}^{\perp},\Gamma_{3}^{\perp},a:B_{1}^{\perp}\invamp B_{2}^{\perp}\vdash s{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}b:=a\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bullet}\,r{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}:A$}}}}\end{array}

3.

l-
\invamp
: The derivation ends in

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{\kern 2.63887pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\vdash{\Gamma_{1},B_{1},B_{2}}$}}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=58.93741pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{l-$\invamp$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\vdash{\Gamma_{1},B_{1}\invamp B_{2}}$}}}}\end{array}

There are two cases depending on whether
A=B_{1}\invamp B_{2}
or
A\in\Gamma_{1}
.

(a)

A=B_{1}\invamp B_{2}
. By the IH,
\cdot;\overline{a}:\Gamma_{1}^{\perp},b:B_{1}\vdash s:B_{2}
. We conclude from the derivation:

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\begin{array}[]{c}\enskip{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\begin{array}[]{c}\text{IH }\\
\cdot;\overline{a}:\Gamma_{1}^{\perp},b:B_{1}^{\perp}\vdash s:B_{2}\end{array}\quad\cdot;c:B_{2}^{\perp}\vdash c:B_{2}^{\perp}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=164.27295pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{}$}}}\hbox{\kern 14.0384pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\cdot;\overline{a}:\Gamma_{1}^{\perp},b:B_{1}^{\perp},c:B_{2}^{\perp}\vdash s\lightning c:{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=179.27295pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-i$\invamp$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 22.3855pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\cdot;\overline{a}:\Gamma_{1}^{\perp}\vdash{\invamp}(b,c).s\lightning c:B_{1}\invamp B_{2}$}}}}\end{array}

Thus it suffices to take
t:={\invamp}(b,c).s\lightning c
.

(b)

A\in\Gamma_{1}
. Thus
\Gamma_{1}=\Gamma_{2},A
. By the IH
\cdot;\overline{a}:\Gamma_{2}^{\perp},b:B_{1}^{\perp},c:B_{2}^{\perp}\vdash s:A
. Then we construct the following derivation and conclude:

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\cdot;d:B_{1}^{\perp}\otimes B_{2}^{\perp}\vdash d:B_{1}^{\perp}\otimes B_{2}^{\perp}\quad\begin{array}[]{c}\text{IH }\\
\cdot;\overline{a}:\Gamma_{2}^{\perp},b:B_{1}^{\perp},c:B_{2}^{\perp}\vdash s:A\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=260.12523pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-e$\otimes$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 52.25665pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\cdot;d:B_{1}^{\perp}\otimes B_{2}^{\perp},\overline{a}:\Gamma_{2}^{\perp}\vdash{s}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{[}}\langle b,c\rangle:=d{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{]}}:A$}}}}\end{array}

Thus it suffices to take
t:={s}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{[}}\langle b,c\rangle:=d{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{]}}
.

4.

l-!p
: The derivation ends in

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{\kern 0.55557pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\vdash{{?}\Gamma_{1},B}$}}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=37.98956pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{l-!p}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\vdash{{?}\Gamma_{1},{!}{B}}$}}}}\end{array}

There are two cases depending on whether
A={!}{B}
or
A\in{?}\Gamma_{1}
.

(a)

A={!}{B}
. By the IH
\cdot;\overline{a}:{!}{\Gamma_{1}^{\perp}}\vdash s:B
. By Lem.
32
(
2
), we have
\overline{u}:\Gamma_{1}^{\perp};\cdot\vdash s{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}\overline{a}:=\overline{{!}{u}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}:B
. We thus derive:

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{\kern 3.88898pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\overline{u}:\Gamma_{1}^{\perp};\cdot\vdash s{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}\overline{a}:=\overline{{!}{u}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}:B$}}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=93.66333pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-i${!}{}$}'}$}}}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\overline{u}:\Gamma_{1}^{\perp};\cdot\vdash{!}{(s{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}\overline{a}:=\overline{{!}{u}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}})}:{!}{B}$}}}}\end{array}

Finally, from Lem.
32
(
1
), we obtain
\cdot;\overline{b}:{!}{\Gamma_{1}^{\perp}}\vdash{!}{(s{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}\overline{a}:=\overline{{!}{u}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}})}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{[}}{!}{\overline{u}}:=\overline{b}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{]}}:{!}{B}
. Thus it suffices to take
t:={!}{(s{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}\overline{a}:=\overline{{!}{u}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}})}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{[}}{!}{\overline{u}}:=\overline{b}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{]}}
.

(b)

A\in{?}\Gamma_{1}
. Thus
{?}\Gamma_{1}={?}\Gamma_{2},{?}A_{1}
and
A={?}A_{1}
. By the IH
\cdot;\overline{a}:{!}{\Gamma_{1}^{\perp}},b:B^{\perp}\vdash s:{?}A_{1}
. By Lem.
32
(
2
), we have
\overline{u}:\Gamma_{1}^{\perp};b:B^{\perp}\vdash s{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}\overline{a}:=\overline{{!}{u}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}:{?}A_{1}
.

By Weakening we have
v:A_{1}^{\perp},\overline{u}:\Gamma_{1}^{\perp};b:B^{\perp}\vdash s{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}\overline{a}:=\overline{{!}{u}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}:{?}A_{1}
. We reason as follows

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\begin{array}[]{c}\enskip{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty v:A_{1}^{\perp},\overline{u}:\Gamma_{1}^{\perp};c:{?}B^{\perp}\vdash c:{?}B^{\perp}\quad\begin{array}[]{c}\enskip{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{\kern 25.14783pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\begin{array}[]{l}v:A_{1}^{\perp},\overline{u}:\Gamma_{1}^{\perp};b:B^{\perp}\vdash s{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}\overline{a}:=\overline{{!}{u}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}:{?}A_{1}\\
v:A_{1}^{\perp},\overline{u}:\Gamma_{1}^{\perp};\cdot\vdash{!}{d.(d\lightning v)}:{!}{A_{1}^{\perp}}\end{array}$}}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=188.60953pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{}$}}}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle v:A_{1}^{\perp},\overline{u}:\Gamma_{1}^{\perp};b:B^{\perp}\vdash s{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}\overline{a}:=\overline{{!}{u}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}\lightning{!}{d.(d\lightning v)}:{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=335.94952pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-e${?}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 60.72987pt\hbox{$\displaystyle v:A_{1}^{\perp},\overline{u}:\Gamma_{1}^{\perp};c:{?}B^{\perp}\vdash{(s{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}\overline{a}:=\overline{{!}{u}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}\lightning{!}{d.(d\lightning v)}){\color[rgb]{0,0.6,0}\blacktriangleright}^{b}c}:{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=371.78288pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-i${?}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 82.47285pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\overline{u}:\Gamma_{1}^{\perp};c:{?}B^{\perp}\vdash{?}v.{(s{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}\overline{a}:=\overline{{!}{u}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}\lightning{!}{d.(d\lightning v)}){\color[rgb]{0,0.6,0}\blacktriangleright}^{b}c}:{?}A_{1}$}}}}\end{array}

Finally, from
\overline{u}:\Gamma_{1}^{\perp};c:{?}B^{\perp}\vdash{?}v.{(s{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}\overline{a}:=\overline{{!}{u}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}\lightning{!}{d.(d\lightning v)}){\color[rgb]{0,0.6,0}\blacktriangleright}^{b}c}:{?}A_{1}
and Lem.
32
(
2
), we have
\cdot;\overline{d}:{!}{\Gamma_{1}^{\perp}},c:{?}B^{\perp}\vdash{?}v.{(s{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}\overline{a}:=\overline{{!}{u}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}\lightning{!}{d.(d\lightning v)}){\color[rgb]{0,0.6,0}\blacktriangleright}^{b}c}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{[}}{!}{\overline{u}}:=\overline{d}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{]}}:{?}A_{1}
. Thus it suffices to take
t:={?}v.{(s{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}\overline{a}:=\overline{{!}{u}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}\lightning{!}{d.(d\lightning v)}){\color[rgb]{0,0.6,0}\blacktriangleright}^{b}c}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{[}}{!}{\overline{u}}:=\overline{d}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{]}}
.

5.

l-?w
: The derivation ends in

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{\kern 7.79341pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\vdash{\Gamma_{1}}$}}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=35.21179pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{l-?w}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\vdash{\Gamma_{1},{?}B}$}}}}\end{array}

There are two cases depending on whether
A={?}B
or
A\in\Gamma_{1}
.

(a)

A={?}B
. Note that
\Gamma_{1}
cannot be empty since
MELL
is consistent. Let
\Gamma_{1}=\Gamma_{2},C
. By the IH
\cdot;\overline{a}:\Gamma_{2}^{\perp}\vdash s:C
. By weakening (Lem.
30
)
u:B^{\perp};\overline{a}:\Gamma_{2}^{\perp}\vdash s:C
. Then we can construct the derivation below and conclude:

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\begin{array}[]{c}\enskip{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty u:B^{\perp};\overline{a}:\Gamma_{2}^{\perp}\vdash s:C\quad u:B^{\perp};c:C^{\perp}\vdash c:C^{\perp}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=186.77428pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-i${\perp}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 29.40695pt\hbox{$\displaystyle u:B^{\perp};\overline{a}:\Gamma_{2}^{\perp},c:C^{\perp}\vdash s\lightning c:{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=223.71875pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-i${?}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 50.61523pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\cdot;\overline{a}:\Gamma_{2}^{\perp},c:C^{\perp}\vdash{?}u.s\lightning c:{?}B$}}}}\end{array}

Thus it suffices to take
t:={?}u.s\lightning c
.

(b)

A\in\Gamma_{1}
. Then
\Gamma_{1}=\Gamma_{2},A
. By the IH
\cdot;\overline{a}:\Gamma_{2}^{\perp}\vdash t:A
. By weakening Lem.
30

u:B^{\perp};\overline{a}:\Gamma_{2}^{\perp}\vdash t:A
. By Lem.
32
(
1
),
\cdot;b:{!}{B^{\perp}},\overline{a}:\Gamma_{2}^{\perp}\vdash t{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{[}}{!}{u}:=b{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{]}}:A
. We thus conclude.

6.

l-?d
: The derivation ends in

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{\kern 1.5278pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\vdash{\Gamma_{1},B}$}}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=35.21179pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{l-?d}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\vdash{\Gamma_{1},{?}B}$}}}}\end{array}

There are two cases depending on whether
A={?}B
or
A\in\Gamma_{1}
.

(a)

A={?}B
. By the IH
\cdot;\overline{a}:\Gamma_{1}^{\perp}\vdash s:B
. By weakening
u:B^{\perp};\overline{a}:\Gamma_{1}^{\perp}\vdash s:B
. We conclude as follows:

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\begin{array}[]{c}\enskip{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty u:B^{\perp};\overline{a}:\Gamma_{1}^{\perp}\vdash s:B\quad\begin{array}[]{c}\enskip{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{\kern 35.11679pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty$}}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=70.23358pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-uax}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle u:B^{\perp};\cdot\vdash u:B^{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=205.95792pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-i${\perp}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 53.6591pt\hbox{$\displaystyle u:B^{\perp};\overline{a}:\Gamma_{1}^{\perp}\vdash s\lightning u:{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=242.90239pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-i${?}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 74.86737pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\cdot;\overline{a}:\Gamma_{1}^{\perp}\vdash{?}u.s\lightning u:{?}B$}}}}\end{array}

Thus it suffices to take
t:={?}u.s\lightning u
.

(b)

A\in\Gamma_{1}
. Then
\Gamma_{1}=\Gamma_{2},A
. By the IH
\cdot;\overline{a}:\Gamma_{2}^{\perp},b:B^{\perp}\vdash s:A
. By weakening Lem.
30
,
u:B^{\perp};\overline{a}:\Gamma_{2}^{\perp},b:B^{\perp}\vdash s:A
. Then we obtain
u:B^{\perp};\overline{a}:\Gamma_{2}^{\perp}\vdash s{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}b:=u{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}:A
as follows:

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty u:B^{\perp};\cdot\vdash u:B^{\perp}\quad u:B^{\perp};\overline{a}:\Gamma_{2}^{\perp},b:B^{\perp}\vdash s:A$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=193.77713pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-sub}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 36.67285pt\hbox{$\displaystyle u:B^{\perp};\overline{a}:\Gamma_{2}^{\perp},\vdash s{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}b:=u{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}:A$}}}}\end{array}

By Lem.
32
(
1
),
\cdot;b:{!}{B^{\perp}},\overline{a}:\Gamma_{2}^{\perp}\vdash s{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}b:=u{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{[}}{!}{u}:=b{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{]}}:A
.
We thus conclude, taking
t:=s{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}b:=u{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{[}}{!}{u}:=b{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{]}}
.

7.

l-?c
: The derivation ends in

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\vdash{\Gamma_{1},{?}B,{?}B}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=50.79861pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{l-?c}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 7.79341pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\vdash{\Gamma_{1},{?}B}$}}}}\end{array}

There are two cases depending on whether
A={?}B
or
A\in\Gamma_{1}
.

(a)

A={?}B
. By the IH
\cdot;\overline{a}:\Gamma_{1}^{\perp},b:{!}{B^{\perp}}\vdash s:{?}B
. By Lem.
32
(
2
),
u:B^{\perp};\overline{a}:\Gamma_{1}^{\perp}\vdash s{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}a:={!}{u}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}:{?}B
. We then construct the following derivation and conclude:

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\begin{array}[]{c}\enskip{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty u:B^{\perp};\overline{a}:\Gamma_{1}^{\perp}\vdash s{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}a:={!}{u}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}:{?}B\quad u:B^{\perp};\cdot\vdash{!}{u}:{!}{B^{\perp}}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=196.96867pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-i${\perp}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 28.10359pt\hbox{$\displaystyle u:B^{\perp};\overline{a}:\Gamma_{1}^{\perp}\vdash(s{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}a:={!}{u}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}})\lightning{!}{u}:{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=233.91315pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-i${?}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 49.31189pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\cdot;\overline{a}:\Gamma_{1}^{\perp}\vdash{?}u.(s{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}a:={!}{u}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}})\lightning{!}{u}:{?}B\par$}}}}\end{array}

Thus it suffices to take
t:={?}u.(s{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}a:={!}{u}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}})\lightning{!}{u}
.

(b)

A\in\Gamma_{1}
. Then
\Gamma_{1}=\Gamma_{2},A
. We reason as follows

\begin{array}[]{rl}\cdot;\overline{a}:\Gamma_{2}^{\perp},b_{1}:{!}{B^{\perp}},b_{2}:{!}{B^{\perp}}\vdash s:A&IH\\
u_{1}:B^{\perp},u_{2}:B^{\perp};\overline{a}:\Gamma_{2}^{\perp}\vdash s{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}\overline{b}:=\overline{{!}{u}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}:A&Lem.~\ref{lemma:unrestricted_eq_bang_linear}(\ref{from_lin})\\
u_{1}:B^{\perp};\overline{a}:\Gamma_{2}^{\perp}\vdash(s{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}\overline{b}:=\overline{{!}{u}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}u_{2}:=u_{1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}:A&Lem.~\ref{lemma:weakening_and_contraction}\\
\cdot;\overline{a}:\Gamma_{2}^{\perp},b:{!}{B^{\perp}}\vdash(s{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}\overline{b}:=\overline{{!}{u}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}u_{2}:=u_{1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{[}}{!}{u_{1}}:=b{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{]}}:A&Lem.~\ref{lemma:unrestricted_eq_bang_linear}(\ref{to_lin})\end{array}

8.

l-
1
. The derivation ends in:

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{\kern 3.62495pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\vphantom{{}^{@}}$}}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=13.88882pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{l-$\text{1}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\vdash{\text{1}}$}}}}\end{array}

We then take:

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{\kern 11.40277pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\vphantom{{}^{@}}$}}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=29.44446pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-i$\text{1}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\cdot;\cdot\vdash\star:\text{1}$}}}}\end{array}

9.

l-
{\perp}
. The derivation ends in:

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{\kern 5.1389pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\vdash{\Gamma}$}}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=25.41664pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{l-${\perp}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\vdash{\Gamma,{\perp}}$}}}}\end{array}

We consider two cases depending on whether
A={\perp}
or
A\in\Gamma
.

(a)

A={\perp}
. Since
MELL
is consistent,
\Gamma
cannot be empty. Thus
\Gamma=\Gamma_{1},B
.

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\begin{array}[]{c}\text{IH }\\
\cdot;\Gamma_{1}^{\perp}\vdash s:B\end{array}\quad\cdot;a:B^{\perp}\vdash a:B^{\perp}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=140.2414pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-i${\perp}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 20.96179pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\cdot;\Gamma_{1}^{\perp},a:B^{\perp}\vdash s\lightning a:{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}

Thus it suffices to take
t:=s\lightning a
.

(b)

A\in\Gamma
. Then
\Gamma=\Gamma_{1},A
.

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\cdot;a:\text{1}\vdash a:\text{1}\quad\begin{array}[]{c}\text{IH }\\
\cdot;\Gamma_{1}^{\perp}\vdash s:A\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=118.64777pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-e$\text{1}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 10.13884pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\cdot;\Gamma_{1}^{\perp},a:\text{1}\vdash s{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{[}}\star:=a{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{]}}:A$}}}}\end{array}

Thus it suffices to take
t:=s{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{[}}\star:=a{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{]}}
.

∎

\ContrasubstitutionLemma

*

Proof.

By induction on the size of the derivation of
\Delta;\Gamma_{1},a:A\vdash t:B
.
We omit the cases already addressed in

A
and detail only the new cases below.

1.

Case
m-ax
.

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{\kern 30.0218pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\vphantom{{}^{@}}$}}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=66.68253pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-ax}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\Delta;a:A\vdash a:A$}}}}\end{array}

Then
B=A
and we conclude from the hypothesis.

2.

Case
m-uax
.
This case is not possible since
a
does not occur in
u
.

3.

Case
m-e
\otimes
. There are two cases.

(a)

a:A
is used to type
t_{2}
.

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\Delta;\Gamma_{11},a:A\vdash t_{2}:C\otimes D\quad\Delta;\Gamma_{12},b:C,c:D\vdash t_{1}:B$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=227.96889pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-e$\otimes$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 38.22472pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\Delta;\Gamma_{11},a:A,\Gamma_{12}\vdash t_{1}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{[}}\langle b,c\rangle:=t_{2}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{]}}:B$}}}}\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\Delta;\Gamma_{11},a:A\vdash t_{2}:C\otimes D\quad\begin{array}[]{c}\enskip{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\begin{array}[]{c}\enskip{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\Delta;\Gamma_{12},b:C,c:D\vdash t_{1}:B\quad\Delta;\Gamma_{2}\vdash s:B^{\perp}\par$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=180.20473pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-i${\perp}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 21.2395pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\Delta;\Gamma_{12},b:C,c:D,\Gamma_{2}\vdash t_{1}\lightning s:{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=217.1492pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-i$\invamp$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 33.2672pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\Delta;\Gamma_{12},\Gamma_{2}\vdash{\invamp}(b,c).t_{1}\lightning s:C^{\perp}\invamp D^{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=371.1181pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{}$}}}\hbox{\kern 95.61696pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\Delta;\Gamma_{11},\Gamma_{12},\Gamma_{2}\vdash t_{2}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\{}}a\backslash\!\!\backslash{\invamp}(b,c).t_{1}\lightning s{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\}}}:A^{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}

(b)

a:A
is used to type
t_{1}
.

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\Delta;\Gamma_{11}\vdash t_{2}:C\otimes D\quad\Delta;\Gamma_{12},a:A,b:C,c:D\vdash t_{1}:B$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=225.19118pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-e$\otimes$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 35.447pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\Delta;\Gamma_{11},\Gamma_{12},a:A\vdash t_{1}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{[}}\langle b,c\rangle:=t_{2}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{]}}:B$}}}}\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\Delta;\Gamma_{11}\vdash t_{2}:C\otimes D\quad\begin{array}[]{c}\enskip{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\Delta;\Gamma_{12},a:A,b:C,c:D\vdash t_{1}:B\quad\Delta;\Gamma_{2}\vdash s:B^{\perp}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=205.76825pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{}$}}}\hbox{\kern 21.97552pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\Delta;\Gamma_{12},b:C,c:D,\Gamma_{2}\vdash t_{1}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\{}}a\backslash\!\!\backslash s{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\}}}:A^{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=309.45145pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-e$\otimes$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 58.3422pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\Delta;\Gamma_{11},\Gamma_{12},a:A\vdash t_{1}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\{}}a\backslash\!\!\backslash s{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\}}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{[}}\langle b,c\rangle:=t_{2}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{]}}:A^{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}

4.

Case
m-i
\invamp

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{\kern 3.0695pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\Delta;\Gamma_{1},a:A,b:B_{1},c:B_{2}\vdash t:{\perp}$}}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=140.09221pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-i$\invamp$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\Delta;\Gamma_{1},a:A\vdash{\invamp}(b,c).t:B_{1}^{\perp}\invamp B_{2}^{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\Delta;\Gamma_{2}\vdash s:B_{1}\otimes B_{2}\quad\begin{array}[]{c}\enskip{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\Delta;\Gamma_{1},a:A,b:B_{1},c:B_{2}\vdash t:{\perp}\quad\Delta;\cdot\vdash\star:\text{1}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=176.73108pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{}$}}}\hbox{\kern 15.59717pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\Delta;\Gamma_{1},b:B_{1},c:B_{2}\vdash t{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\{}}a\backslash\!\!\backslash\star{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\}}}:A^{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=282.30038pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-e$\otimes$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 67.82617pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\Delta;\Gamma_{1},\Gamma_{2}\vdash t{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\{}}a\backslash\!\!\backslash\star{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\}}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{[}}\langle b,c\rangle:=s{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{]}}:A^{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}

5.

Case
m-i
{!}{}
. This case is not possible since
\Gamma_{1},a:A
is not the empty context.

6.

Case
m-e
{!}{}
. Then
t=t_{1}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{[}}{!}{u}:=t_{2}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{]}}
for some
t_{1},t_{2}
and
u
. There are two cases depending on whether
a:A
is used to type
t_{1}
or
t_{2}
.

(a)

a:A
is used to type
t_{1}
. Then
\Gamma_{1}=\Gamma_{11},\Gamma_{12}
and the derivation ends in

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\Delta;\Gamma_{11}\vdash t_{2}:{!}{C}\quad\Delta,u:C;\Gamma_{12},a:A\vdash t_{1}:B$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=180.73853pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-e${!}{}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 19.39021pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\Delta;\Gamma_{11},\Gamma_{12},a:A\vdash t_{1}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{[}}{!}{u}:=t_{2}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{]}}:B$}}}}\end{array}

By weakening (Lem.
30
),
\Delta,u:C;\Gamma_{2}\vdash s:B^{\perp}
. Thus we use the IH to construct the derivation:

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\Delta;\Gamma_{11}\vdash t_{2}:{!}{C}\quad\begin{array}[]{c}\enskip{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\Delta,u:C;\Gamma_{12},a:A\vdash t_{1}:B\quad\Delta,u:C;\Gamma_{1}\vdash s:B^{\perp}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=208.45929pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-cos}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 35.15787pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\Delta,u:C;\Gamma_{12},\Gamma_{2}\vdash t_{1}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\{}}a\backslash\!\!\backslash s{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\}}}:A^{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=316.41907pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-e${!}{}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 74.57587pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\Delta;\Gamma_{11},\Gamma_{12},\Gamma_{2}\vdash t_{1}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\{}}a\backslash\!\!\backslash s{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\}}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{[}}{!}{u}:=t_{2}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{]}}:A^{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}

(b)

a:A
is used to type
t_{2}
.

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\Delta;\Gamma_{11},a:A\vdash t_{2}:{!}{C}\quad\Delta,u:C;\Gamma_{12}\vdash t_{1}:B$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=180.73853pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-e${!}{}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 19.39021pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\Delta;\Gamma_{11},\Gamma_{12},a:A\vdash t_{1}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{[}}{!}{u}:=t_{2}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{]}}:B$}}}}\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\Delta;\Gamma_{11},a:A\vdash t_{2}:{!}{C}\quad\begin{array}[]{c}\enskip{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\begin{array}[]{c}\enskip{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\Delta,u:C;\Gamma_{12}\vdash t_{1}:B\quad\Delta,u:C;\Gamma_{2}\vdash s:B^{\perp}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=180.11806pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-i${\perp}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 33.03299pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\Delta,u:C;\Gamma_{12},\Gamma_{2}\vdash t_{1}\lightning s:{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=217.06253pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-i${?}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 53.21347pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\Delta;\Gamma_{12},\Gamma_{2}\vdash{?}u.t_{1}\lightning s:{?}C^{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=347.47466pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-cos}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 92.74254pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\Delta;\Gamma_{11},\Gamma_{12},\Gamma_{2}\vdash t_{2}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\{}}a\backslash\!\!\backslash{?}u.t_{1}\lightning s{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\}}}:A^{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\Delta;\Gamma_{2}\vdash s:\text{1}\quad\begin{array}[]{c}\enskip{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\Delta;\Gamma_{12},a:A\vdash t_{2}:C\quad\Delta;\Gamma_{11}\vdash t_{1}:C^{\perp}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=159.89944pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{m-i${\perp}$}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 20.36235pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\Delta;\Gamma_{11},\Gamma_{12}\vdash t_{2}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\{}}a\backslash\!\!\backslash t_{1}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\}}}:A^{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=254.48969pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{}$}}}\hbox{\kern 45.36235pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\Delta;\Gamma_{11},\Gamma_{12},\Gamma_{2}\vdash t_{2}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\{}}a\backslash\!\!\backslash t_{1}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\}}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{[}}\star:=s{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{]}}:A^{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}

.
