{\perp}
-I

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{\kern 15.70255pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\Delta;\Gamma\vdash M:A\mid\Sigma$}}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=102.61308pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{${\perp}$-I}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\Delta;\Gamma\vdash[a]M:{\perp}\mid\Sigma,a:A$}}}}\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{\kern 28.71631pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty{\Delta}^{\mathsf{H}};{\Gamma}^{\mathsf{H}},({\Sigma}^{\mathsf{H}})^{\perp}\vdash{M}^{\mathsf{H}}:{A}^{\mathsf{H}}$}}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=154.46059pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{}$}}}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\Delta}^{\mathsf{H}};{\Gamma}^{\mathsf{H}},({\Sigma}^{\mathsf{H}})^{\perp},a:({A}^{\mathsf{H}})^{\perp}\vdash{M}^{\mathsf{H}}\lightning a:{\perp}$}}}}\end{array}

8.

{\perp}
-E

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty\Delta;\Gamma\vdash M:{\perp}\mid\Sigma,a:A$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=91.77162pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{{${\perp}$-E}}$}}}\hbox{\kern 2.40392pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\Delta;\Gamma\vdash\mu{a}.M:A\mid\Sigma$}}}}\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{c}{\vbox{\hbox spread0.0pt{\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\penalty{\Delta}^{\mathsf{H}};{\Gamma}^{\mathsf{H}},({\Sigma}^{\mathsf{H}})^{\perp},a:({A}^{\mathsf{H}})^{\perp}\vdash{M}^{\mathsf{H}}:{\perp}$}\hskip 0.0pt}\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\vrule height=2.75002pt,depth=-2.24998pt,width=136.11928pt\hbox{\kern 3.00003pt$\textsc{}$}}}\hbox{\kern 3.84712pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\Delta}^{\mathsf{H}},({\Sigma}^{\mathsf{H}})^{\perp};{\Gamma}^{\mathsf{H}}\vdash{M}^{\mathsf{H}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\{}}a\backslash\!\!\backslash\star{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\}}}:{A}^{\mathsf{H}}$}}}}\end{array}

∎

Lemma 86

(
{(\cdot)}^{\mathsf{H}}
commutes with linear and unrestricted substitution)
.

{M{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}x:=N{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}}^{\mathsf{H}}={M}^{\mathsf{H}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}x:={N}^{\mathsf{H}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}
and
{M{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}u:=N{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}}^{\mathsf{H}}={M}^{\mathsf{H}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}u:={N}^{\mathsf{H}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}
.

Proof.

By induction on
M
using Lem.
41
.
∎

Lemma 87
.

{((\Lambda x.\,M)\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{@}}\,N)}^{\mathsf{H}}\twoheadrightarrow_{\mathsf{\lambda_{\textsf{{MELL}}}}}{(M{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}x:=N{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}})}^{\mathsf{H}}

Proof.

\begin{array}[]{llll }&{((\Lambda x.\,M)\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{@}}\,N)}^{\mathsf{H}}\\
=&{(\Lambda x.\,M)}^{\mathsf{H}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bullet}\,{!}{{N}^{\mathsf{H}}}\\
=&({\invamp}(a,k).({M}^{\mathsf{H}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{[}}{!}{x}:=a{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{]}}\lightning k))\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bullet}\,{!}{{N}^{\mathsf{H}}}\\
\to_{\mathsf{\lambda_{\textsf{{MELL}}}}}&({M}^{\mathsf{H}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{[}}{!}{x}:=a{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{]}}\lightning k){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}a:={!}{{N}^{\mathsf{H}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\{}}k\backslash\!\!\backslash\star{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\}}}\\
=&({M}^{\mathsf{H}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{[}}{!}{x}:={!}{{N}^{\mathsf{H}}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{]}}\lightning k){\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\{}}k\backslash\!\!\backslash\star{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\}}}\\
\equiv&{M}^{\mathsf{H}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{[}}{!}{x}:={!}{{N}^{\mathsf{H}}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{]}}\\
\to_{\mathsf{\lambda_{\textsf{{MELL}}}}}&{M}^{\mathsf{H}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}x:={N}^{\mathsf{H}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}\\
=&{(M{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}x:=N{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}})}^{\mathsf{H}}&Lem.~\ref{lemma:htra_commutes_with_substitution}\end{array}

∎

Lemma 88
.

{((\lambda x.\,M)\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bullet}\,N)}^{\mathsf{H}}\to_{\mathsf{\lambda_{\textsf{{MELL}}}}}{(M{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}x:=N{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}})}^{\mathsf{H}}

Proof.

\begin{array}[]{llll }&{((\lambda x.\,M)\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bullet}\,N)}^{\mathsf{H}}\\
=&{(\lambda x.\,M)}^{\mathsf{H}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bullet}\,{N}^{\mathsf{H}}\\
=&({\invamp}(x,k).({M}^{\mathsf{H}}\lightning k))\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bullet}\,{N}^{\mathsf{H}}\\
\to_{\mathsf{\lambda_{\textsf{{MELL}}}}}&({M}^{\mathsf{H}}\lightning k){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}x:={N}^{\mathsf{H}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\{}}k\backslash\!\!\backslash\star{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\}}}\\
=&({M}^{\mathsf{H}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}x:={N}^{\mathsf{H}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}\lightning k){\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\{}}k\backslash\!\!\backslash\star{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\}}}\\
\equiv&{M}^{\mathsf{H}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}x:={N}^{\mathsf{H}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}}\\
=&{(M{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}x:=N{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}})}^{\mathsf{H}}&Lem.~\ref{lemma:htra_commutes_with_substitution}\\
\end{array}

∎

Lemma 89
.

{\mathsf{C}}\bm{\langle}{t\lightning a}\bm{\rangle}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\{}}a\backslash\!\!\backslash s{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\}}}=t\mathsf{L}
, for some
\mathsf{L}
which depends on
\mathsf{C}
and
s

Proof.

By induction on
\mathsf{C}
.
∎

Lemma 90
.

{(N\,\,(\mu{a}.M))}^{\mathsf{H}}\equiv{(\mu{a}.(M{\color[rgb]{0,0.6,0}\bm{\{}}a{\lhd^{\star}}N{\color[rgb]{0,0.6,0}\bm{\}}}))}^{\mathsf{H}}

Proof.

Since
M
has a unique occurrence of a subterm of the form
[a]O
, it must have the form
{\mathsf{C}}\bm{\langle}{[a]O}\bm{\rangle}
. Thus
{M}^{\mathsf{H}}={{\mathsf{C}}^{\mathsf{H}}}\bm{\langle}{{O}^{\mathsf{H}}\lightning a}\bm{\rangle}
.

\begin{array}[]{llll }&{(N\,\,(\mu{a}.M))}^{\mathsf{H}}\\
=&{N}^{\mathsf{H}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bullet}\,{(\mu{a}.M)}^{\mathsf{H}}\\
=&{N}^{\mathsf{H}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bullet}\,{M}^{\mathsf{H}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\{}}a\backslash\!\!\backslash\star{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\}}}\\
=&{N}^{\mathsf{H}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bullet}\,{{\mathsf{C}}^{\mathsf{H}}}\bm{\langle}{{O}^{\mathsf{H}}\lightning a}\bm{\rangle}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\{}}a\backslash\!\!\backslash\star{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\}}}\\
=&{N}^{\mathsf{H}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bullet}\,{O}^{\mathsf{H}}\mathsf{L}&Lem.~\ref{lemma:contrasubstitution_and_refocussing}\\
\equiv&({N}^{\mathsf{H}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bullet}\,{O}^{\mathsf{H}})\mathsf{L}&Lem.~\ref{lemma:elim_cos_cceq}\\
=&({{\mathsf{C}}^{\mathsf{H}}}\bm{\langle}{({N}^{\mathsf{H}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bullet}\,{O}^{\mathsf{H}})\lightning a}\bm{\rangle}){\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\{}}a\backslash\!\!\backslash\star{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\}}}&Lem.~\ref{lemma:contrasubstitution_and_refocussing}\\
=&{(M{\color[rgb]{0,0.6,0}\bm{\{}}a{\lhd^{\star}}N{\color[rgb]{0,0.6,0}\bm{\}}})}^{\mathsf{H}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\{}}a\backslash\!\!\backslash\star{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\}}}&\\
=&{(\mu{a}.(M{\color[rgb]{0,0.6,0}\bm{\{}}a{\lhd^{\star}}N{\color[rgb]{0,0.6,0}\bm{\}}}))}^{\mathsf{H}}\end{array}

∎

Lemma 91
.

{(\mu{a}.[a]M)}^{\mathsf{H}}\equiv{M}^{\mathsf{H}}
, if
a\notin\mathsf{fv}(M)

Proof.

\begin{array}[]{lll}&{(\mu{a}.[a]M)}^{\mathsf{H}}\\
=&{([a]M)}^{\mathsf{H}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\{}}a\backslash\!\!\backslash\star{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\}}}\\
=&({M}^{\mathsf{H}}\lightning a){\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\{}}a\backslash\!\!\backslash\star{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\}}}\\
=&a{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\{}}a\backslash\!\!\backslash{M}^{\mathsf{H}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{[}}\star:=\star{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{]}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{\}}}\\
=&{M}^{\mathsf{H}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{[}}\star:=\star{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\bm{]}}\\
\equiv&{M}^{\mathsf{H}}\\
\end{array}

∎

Proposition 92
.

If
M\doteq N
, then
{M}^{\mathsf{Q}}_{k}\doteq_{\mathsf{\lambda_{\textsf{{MELL}}}}}{N}^{\mathsf{Q}}_{k}
.

Proof.

By induction on
M\doteq N
, using Lem.
90
, Lem.
88
, Lem.
90
, and Lem.
91
. In summary,

1.

{((\Lambda u.\,M)\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}{@}}\,N)}^{\mathsf{H}}\twoheadrightarrow_{\mathsf{\lambda_{\textsf{{MELL}}}}}{(M{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}u:=N{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}})}^{\mathsf{H}}
.

2.

{((\lambda x.\,M)\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bullet}\,N)}^{\mathsf{H}}\to_{\mathsf{\lambda_{\textsf{{MELL}}}}}{(M{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\{}}x:=N{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bm{\}}})}^{\mathsf{H}}
.

3.

{(N\,\,(\mu{a}.M))}^{\mathsf{H}}\equiv{(\mu{a}.(M{\color[rgb]{0,0.6,0}\bm{\{}}a{\lhd^{\star}}N{\color[rgb]{0,0.6,0}\bm{\}}}))}^{\mathsf{H}}
.

4.

{(\mu{a}.[a]M)}^{\mathsf{H}}\equiv{M}^{\mathsf{H}}
, if
a\notin\mathsf{fv}(M)
.

∎
